## Step 0: Detect platform and base branch

First, detect the git hosting platform from the remote URL:

```bash
git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null
```

- If the URL contains "github.com" → platform is **GitHub**
- If the URL contains "gitlab" → platform is **GitLab**
- Otherwise, check CLI availability:
  - `gh auth status 2>/dev/null` succeeds → platform is **GitHub** (covers GitHub Enterprise)
  - `glab auth status 2>/dev/null` succeeds → platform is **GitLab** (covers self-hosted)
  - Neither → **unknown** (use git-native commands only)

Determine which branch this PR/MR targets, or the repo's default branch if no
PR/MR exists. Use the result as "the base branch" in all subsequent steps.

**If GitHub:**
1. `gh pr view --json baseRefName -q .baseRefName` — if succeeds, use it
2. `gh repo view --json defaultBranchRef -q .defaultBranchRef.name` — if succeeds, use it

**If GitLab:**
1. `glab mr view -F json 2>/dev/null` and extract the `target_branch` field — if succeeds, use it
2. `glab repo view -F json 2>/dev/null` and extract the `default_branch` field — if succeeds, use it

**Git-native fallback (if unknown platform, or CLI commands fail):**
1. `git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD 2>/dev/null | sed 's|refs/remotes/origin/||'`
2. If that fails: `git rev-parse --verify origin/main 2>/dev/null` → use `main`
3. If that fails: `git rev-parse --verify origin/master 2>/dev/null` → use `master`

If all fail, fall back to `main`.

Print the detected base branch name. In every subsequent `git diff`, `git log`,
`git fetch`, `git merge`, and PR/MR creation command, substitute the detected
branch name wherever the instructions say "the base branch" or `<default>`.

# Pre-Landing PR Review

You are running the `/review` workflow. Analyze the current branch's diff against the base branch for structural issues that tests don't catch.

---

## Step 1: Check branch

1. Run `git branch --show-current` to get the current branch.
2. If on the base branch, output: **"Nothing to review — you're on the base branch or have no changes against it."** and stop.
3. Run `git fetch origin <base> --quiet && git diff origin/<base> --stat` to check if there's a diff. If no diff, output the same message and stop.

---

## Step 1.5: Scope Drift Detection

Before reviewing code quality, check: **did they build what was requested — nothing more, nothing less?**

1. Read `TODOS.md` (if it exists). Read PR description (`gh pr view --json body --jq .body 2>/dev/null || true`).
   Read commit messages (`git log origin/<base>..HEAD --oneline`).
   **If no PR exists:** rely on commit messages and TODOS.md for stated intent — this is the common case since /review runs before /ship creates the PR.
2. Identify the **stated intent** — what was this branch supposed to accomplish?
3. Run `git diff origin/<base>...HEAD --stat` and compare the files changed against the stated intent.

4. Evaluate with skepticism (incorporating plan completion results if available from an earlier step or adjacent section):

   **SCOPE CREEP detection:**
   - Files changed that are unrelated to the stated intent
   - New features or refactors not mentioned in the plan
   - "While I was in there..." changes that expand blast radius

   **MISSING REQUIREMENTS detection:**
   - Requirements from TODOS.md/PR description not addressed in the diff
   - Test coverage gaps for stated requirements
   - Partial implementations (started but not finished)

5. Output (before the main review begins):
   \`\`\`
   Scope Check: [CLEAN / DRIFT DETECTED / REQUIREMENTS MISSING]
   Intent: <1-line summary of what was requested>
   Delivered: <1-line summary of what the diff actually does>
   [If drift: list each out-of-scope change]
   [If missing: list each unaddressed requirement]
   \`\`\`

6. This is **INFORMATIONAL** — does not block the review. Proceed to the next step.

---

### Plan File Discovery

1. **Conversation context (primary):** Check if there is an active plan file in this conversation. The host agent's system messages include plan file paths when in plan mode. If found, use it directly — this is the most reliable signal.

2. **Content-based search (fallback):** If no plan file is referenced in conversation context, search by content:

```bash
setopt +o nomatch 2>/dev/null || true  # zsh compat
BRANCH=$(git branch --show-current 2>/dev/null | tr '/' '-')
REPO=$(basename "$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null)")
# Compute project slug for ~/.gstack/projects/ lookup
_PLAN_SLUG=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null | sed 's|.*[:/]\([^/]*/[^/]*\)\.git$|\1|;s|.*[:/]\([^/]*/[^/]*\)$|\1|' | tr '/' '-' | tr -cd 'a-zA-Z0-9._-') || true
_PLAN_SLUG="${_PLAN_SLUG:-$(basename "$PWD" | tr -cd 'a-zA-Z0-9._-')}"
# Search common plan file locations (project designs first, then personal/local)
for PLAN_DIR in "$HOME/.gstack/projects/$_PLAN_SLUG" "$HOME/.claude/plans" "$HOME/.codex/plans" ".gstack/plans"; do
  [ -d "$PLAN_DIR" ] || continue
  PLAN=$(ls -t "$PLAN_DIR"/*.md 2>/dev/null | xargs grep -l "$BRANCH" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  [ -z "$PLAN" ] && PLAN=$(ls -t "$PLAN_DIR"/*.md 2>/dev/null | xargs grep -l "$REPO" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  [ -z "$PLAN" ] && PLAN=$(find "$PLAN_DIR" -name '*.md' -mmin -1440 -maxdepth 1 2>/dev/null | xargs ls -t 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  [ -n "$PLAN" ] && break
done
[ -n "$PLAN" ] && echo "PLAN_FILE: $PLAN" || echo "NO_PLAN_FILE"
```

3. **Validation:** If a plan file was found via content-based search (not conversation context), read the first 20 lines and verify it is relevant to the current branch's work. If it appears to be from a different project or feature, treat as "no plan file found."

**Error handling:**
- No plan file found → skip with "No plan file detected — skipping."
- Plan file found but unreadable (permissions, encoding) → skip with "Plan file found but unreadable — skipping."

### Actionable Item Extraction

Read the plan file. Extract every actionable item — anything that describes work to be done. Look for:

- **Checkbox items:** `- [ ] ...` or `- [x] ...`
- **Numbered steps** under implementation headings: "1. Create ...", "2. Add ...", "3. Modify ..."
- **Imperative statements:** "Add X to Y", "Create a Z service", "Modify the W controller"
- **File-level specifications:** "New file: path/to/file.ts", "Modify path/to/existing.rb"
- **Test requirements:** "Test that X", "Add test for Y", "Verify Z"
- **Data model changes:** "Add column X to table Y", "Create migration for Z"

**Ignore:**
- Context/Background sections (`## Context`, `## Background`, `## Problem`)
- Questions and open items (marked with ?, "TBD", "TODO: decide")
- Review report sections (`## GSTACK REVIEW REPORT`)
- Explicitly deferred items ("Future:", "Out of scope:", "NOT in scope:", "P2:", "P3:", "P4:")
- CEO Review Decisions sections (these record choices, not work items)

**Cap:** Extract at most 50 items. If the plan has more, note: "Showing top 50 of N plan items — full list in plan file."

**No items found:** If the plan contains no extractable actionable items, skip with: "Plan file contains no actionable items — skipping completion audit."

For each item, note:
- The item text (verbatim or concise summary)
- Its category: CODE | TEST | MIGRATION | CONFIG | DOCS

### Cross-Reference Against Diff

Run `git diff origin/<base>...HEAD` and `git log origin/<base>..HEAD --oneline` to understand what was implemented.

For each extracted plan item, check the diff and classify:

- **DONE** — Clear evidence in the diff that this item was implemented. Cite the specific file(s) changed.
- **PARTIAL** — Some work toward this item exists in the diff but it's incomplete (e.g., model created but controller missing, function exists but edge cases not handled).
- **NOT DONE** — No evidence in the diff that this item was addressed.
- **CHANGED** — The item was implemented using a different approach than the plan described, but the same goal is achieved. Note the difference.

**Be conservative with DONE** — require clear evidence in the diff. A file being touched is not enough; the specific functionality described must be present.
**Be generous with CHANGED** — if the goal is met by different means, that counts as addressed.

### Output Format

```
PLAN COMPLETION AUDIT
═══════════════════════════════
Plan: {plan file path}

## Implementation Items
  [DONE]      Create UserService — src/services/user_service.rb (+142 lines)
  [PARTIAL]   Add validation — model validates but missing controller checks
  [NOT DONE]  Add caching layer — no cache-related changes in diff
  [CHANGED]   "Redis queue" → implemented with Sidekiq instead

## Test Items
  [DONE]      Unit tests for UserService — test/services/user_service_test.rb
  [NOT DONE]  E2E test for signup flow

## Migration Items
  [DONE]      Create users table — db/migrate/20240315_create_users.rb

─────────────────────────────────
COMPLETION: 4/7 DONE, 1 PARTIAL, 1 NOT DONE, 1 CHANGED
─────────────────────────────────
```

### Fallback Intent Sources (when no plan file found)

When no plan file is detected, use these secondary intent sources:

1. **Commit messages:** Run `git log origin/<base>..HEAD --oneline`. Use judgment to extract real intent:
   - Commits with actionable verbs ("add", "implement", "fix", "create", "remove", "update") are intent signals
   - Skip noise: "WIP", "tmp", "squash", "merge", "chore", "typo", "fixup"
   - Extract the intent behind the commit, not the literal message
2. **TODOS.md:** If it exists, check for items related to this branch or recent dates
3. **PR description:** Run `gh pr view --json body -q .body 2>/dev/null` for intent context

**With fallback sources:** Apply the same Cross-Reference classification (DONE/PARTIAL/NOT DONE/CHANGED) using best-effort matching. Note that fallback-sourced items are lower confidence than plan-file items.

### Investigation Depth

For each PARTIAL or NOT DONE item, investigate WHY:

1. Check `git log origin/<base>..HEAD --oneline` for commits that suggest the work was started, attempted, or reverted
2. Read the relevant code to understand what was built instead
3. Determine the likely reason from this list:
   - **Scope cut** — evidence of intentional removal (revert commit, removed TODO)
   - **Context exhaustion** — work started but stopped mid-way (partial implementation, no follow-up commits)
   - **Misunderstood requirement** — something was built but it doesn't match what the plan described
   - **Blocked by dependency** — plan item depends on something that isn't available
   - **Genuinely forgotten** — no evidence of any attempt

Output for each discrepancy:
```
DISCREPANCY: {PARTIAL|NOT_DONE} | {plan item} | {what was actually delivered}
INVESTIGATION: {likely reason with evidence from git log / code}
IMPACT: {HIGH|MEDIUM|LOW} — {what breaks or degrades if this stays undelivered}
```

### Learnings Logging (plan-file discrepancies only)

**Only for discrepancies sourced from plan files** (not commit messages or TODOS.md), log a learning so future sessions know this pattern occurred:

```bash
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-learnings-log '{
  "type": "pitfall",
  "key": "plan-delivery-gap-KEBAB_SUMMARY",
  "insight": "Planned X but delivered Y because Z",
  "confidence": 8,
  "source": "observed",
  "files": ["PLAN_FILE_PATH"]
}'
```

Replace KEBAB_SUMMARY with a kebab-case summary of the gap, and fill in the actual values.

**Do NOT log learnings from commit-message-derived or TODOS.md-derived discrepancies.** These are informational in the review output but too noisy for durable memory.

### Integration with Scope Drift Detection

The plan completion results augment the existing Scope Drift Detection. If a plan file is found:

- **NOT DONE items** become additional evidence for **MISSING REQUIREMENTS** in the scope drift report.
- **Items in the diff that don't match any plan item** become evidence for **SCOPE CREEP** detection.
- **HIGH-impact discrepancies** trigger AskUserQuestion:
  - Show the investigation findings
  - Options: A) Stop and implement missing items, B) Ship anyway + create P1 TODOs, C) Intentionally dropped

This is **INFORMATIONAL** unless HIGH-impact discrepancies are found (then it gates via AskUserQuestion).

Update the scope drift output to include plan file context:

```
Scope Check: [CLEAN / DRIFT DETECTED / REQUIREMENTS MISSING]
Intent: <from plan file — 1-line summary>
Plan: <plan file path>
Delivered: <1-line summary of what the diff actually does>
Plan items: N DONE, M PARTIAL, K NOT DONE
[If NOT DONE: list each missing item with investigation]
[If scope creep: list each out-of-scope change not in the plan]
```

**No plan file found:** Use commit messages and TODOS.md as fallback sources (see above). If no intent sources at all, skip with: "No intent sources detected — skipping completion audit."

## Step 2: Read the checklist

Read `.claude/skills/review/checklist.md`.

**If the file cannot be read, STOP and report the error.** Do not proceed without the checklist.

---

## Step 2.5: Check for Greptile review comments

Read `.claude/skills/review/greptile-triage.md` and follow the fetch, filter, classify, and **escalation detection** steps.

**If no PR exists, `gh` fails, API returns an error, or there are zero Greptile comments:** Skip this step silently. Greptile integration is additive — the review works without it.

**If Greptile comments are found:** Store the classifications (VALID & ACTIONABLE, VALID BUT ALREADY FIXED, FALSE POSITIVE, SUPPRESSED) — you will need them in Step 5.

---

## Step 3: Get the diff

Fetch the latest base branch to avoid false positives from stale local state:

```bash
git fetch origin <base> --quiet
```

Run `git diff origin/<base>` to get the full diff. This includes both committed and uncommitted changes against the latest base branch.

## Step 3.4: Workspace-aware queue status (advisory)

Check whether this PR's claimed VERSION still points at a free slot in the queue. Advisory only — never blocks review; just informs the reviewer about landing-order risk.

```bash
BRANCH_VERSION=$(git show HEAD:VERSION 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r\n[:space:]' || echo "")
BASE_BRANCH=$(gh pr view --json baseRefName -q .baseRefName 2>/dev/null || echo main)
BASE_VERSION=$(git show origin/$BASE_BRANCH:VERSION 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r\n[:space:]' || echo "")
QUEUE_JSON=$(bun run bin/gstack-next-version \
  --base "$BASE_BRANCH" \
  --bump patch \
  --current-version "$BASE_VERSION" 2>/dev/null || echo '{"offline":true}')
NEXT_SLOT=$(echo "$QUEUE_JSON" | jq -r '.version // empty')
CLAIMED_COUNT=$(echo "$QUEUE_JSON" | jq -r '.claimed | length // 0')
OFFLINE=$(echo "$QUEUE_JSON" | jq -r '.offline // false')
```

- If `OFFLINE=true`: skip this section (no signal to report).
- Otherwise, include ONE line in the review output: `Version claimed: v<BRANCH_VERSION>. Queue: <CLAIMED_COUNT> PR(s) ahead. <VERDICT>` where VERDICT is either `Slot free` (if `BRANCH_VERSION >= NEXT_SLOT`) or `⚠ queue moved — rerun /ship to reconcile v<BRANCH_VERSION> → v<NEXT_SLOT>`.

---

## Step 3.5: Slop scan (advisory)

Run a slop scan on changed files to catch AI code quality issues (empty catches,
redundant `return await`, overcomplicated abstractions):

```bash
bun run slop:diff origin/<base> 2>/dev/null || true
```

If findings are reported, include them in the review output as an informational
diagnostic. Slop findings are advisory, never blocking. If slop:diff is not
available (e.g., slop-scan not installed), skip this step silently.

---

## Prior Learnings

Search for relevant learnings from previous sessions:

```bash
_CROSS_PROJ=$(~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config get cross_project_learnings 2>/dev/null || echo "unset")
echo "CROSS_PROJECT: $_CROSS_PROJ"
if [ "$_CROSS_PROJ" = "true" ]; then
  ~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-learnings-search --limit 10 --cross-project 2>/dev/null || true
else
  ~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-learnings-search --limit 10 2>/dev/null || true
fi
```

If `CROSS_PROJECT` is `unset` (first time): Use AskUserQuestion:

> gstack can search learnings from your other projects on this machine to find
> patterns that might apply here. This stays local (no data leaves your machine).
> Recommended for solo developers. Skip if you work on multiple client codebases
> where cross-contamination would be a concern.

Options:
- A) Enable cross-project learnings (recommended)
- B) Keep learnings project-scoped only

If A: run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set cross_project_learnings true`
If B: run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set cross_project_learnings false`

Then re-run the search with the appropriate flag.

If learnings are found, incorporate them into your analysis. When a review finding
matches a past learning, display:

**"Prior learning applied: [key] (confidence N/10, from [date])"**

This makes the compounding visible. The user should see that gstack is getting
smarter on their codebase over time.

## Step 4: Critical pass (core review)

Apply the CRITICAL categories from the checklist against the diff:
SQL & Data Safety, Race Conditions & Concurrency, LLM Output Trust Boundary, Shell Injection, Enum & Value Completeness.

Also apply the remaining INFORMATIONAL categories that are still in the checklist (Async/Sync Mixing, Column/Field Name Safety, LLM Prompt Issues, Type Coercion, View/Frontend, Time Window Safety, Completeness Gaps, Distribution & CI/CD).

**Enum & Value Completeness requires reading code OUTSIDE the diff.** When the diff introduces a new enum value, status, tier, or type constant, use Grep to find all files that reference sibling values, then Read those files to check if the new value is handled. This is the one category where within-diff review is insufficient.

**Search-before-recommending:** When recommending a fix pattern (especially for concurrency, caching, auth, or framework-specific behavior):
- Verify the pattern is current best practice for the framework version in use
- Check if a built-in solution exists in newer versions before recommending a workaround
- Verify API signatures against current docs (APIs change between versions)

Takes seconds, prevents recommending outdated patterns. If WebSearch is unavailable, note it and proceed with in-distribution knowledge.

Follow the output format specified in the checklist. Respect the suppressions — do NOT flag items listed in the "DO NOT flag" section.

## Confidence Calibration

Every finding MUST include a confidence score (1-10):

| Score | Meaning | Display rule |
|-------|---------|-------------|
| 9-10 | Verified by reading specific code. Concrete bug or exploit demonstrated. | Show normally |
| 7-8 | High confidence pattern match. Very likely correct. | Show normally |
| 5-6 | Moderate. Could be a false positive. | Show with caveat: "Medium confidence, verify this is actually an issue" |
| 3-4 | Low confidence. Pattern is suspicious but may be fine. | Suppress from main report. Include in appendix only. |
| 1-2 | Speculation. | Only report if severity would be P0. |

**Finding format:**

\`[SEVERITY] (confidence: N/10) file:line — description\`

Example:
\`[P1] (confidence: 9/10) app/models/user.rb:42 — SQL injection via string interpolation in where clause\`
\`[P2] (confidence: 5/10) app/controllers/api/v1/users_controller.rb:18 — Possible N+1 query, verify with production logs\`

**Calibration learning:** If you report a finding with confidence < 7 and the user
confirms it IS a real issue, that is a calibration event. Your initial confidence was
too low. Log the corrected pattern as a learning so future reviews catch it with
higher confidence.

---

## Step 4.5: Review Army — Specialist Dispatch

### Detect stack and scope

```bash
source <(~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-diff-scope <base> 2>/dev/null) || true
# Detect stack for specialist context
STACK=""
[ -f Gemfile ] && STACK="${STACK}ruby "
[ -f package.json ] && STACK="${STACK}node "
[ -f requirements.txt ] || [ -f pyproject.toml ] && STACK="${STACK}python "
[ -f go.mod ] && STACK="${STACK}go "
4. Instructions:

"You are a specialist code reviewer. Read the checklist below, then run
`git diff origin/<base>` to get the full diff. Apply the checklist against the diff.

For each finding, output a JSON object on its own line:
{\"severity\":\"CRITICAL|INFORMATIONAL\",\"confidence\":N,\"path\":\"file\",\"line\":N,\"category\":\"category\",\"summary\":\"description\",\"fix\":\"recommended fix\",\"fingerprint\":\"path:line:category\",\"specialist\":\"name\"}

Required fields: severity, confidence, path, category, summary, specialist.
Optional: line, fix, fingerprint, evidence, test_stub.

If you can write a test that would catch this issue, include it in the `test_stub` field.
Use the detected test framework ({TEST_FW}). Write a minimal skeleton — describe/it/test
blocks with clear intent. Skip test_stub for architectural or design-only findings.

If no findings: output `NO FINDINGS` and nothing else.
Do not output anything else — no preamble, no summary, no commentary.

Stack context: {STACK}
Past learnings: {learnings or 'none'}

CHECKLIST:
{checklist content}"

**Subagent configuration:**
- Use `subagent_type: "general-purpose"`
- Do NOT use `run_in_background` — all specialists must complete before merge
- If any specialist subagent fails or times out, log the failure and continue with results from successful specialists. Specialists are additive — partial results are better than no results.

---

### Step 4.6: Collect and merge findings

After all specialist subagents complete, collect their outputs.

**Parse findings:**
For each specialist's output:
1. If output is "NO FINDINGS" — skip, this specialist found nothing
2. Otherwise, parse each line as a JSON object. Skip lines that are not valid JSON.
3. Collect all parsed findings into a single list, tagged with their specialist name.

**Fingerprint and deduplicate:**
For each finding, compute its fingerprint:
- If `fingerprint` field is present, use it
- Otherwise: `{path}:{line}:{category}` (if line is present) or `{path}:{category}`

Group findings by fingerprint. For findings sharing the same fingerprint:
- Keep the finding with the highest confidence score
- Tag it: "MULTI-SPECIALIST CONFIRMED ({specialist1} + {specialist2})"
- Boost confidence by +1 (cap at 10)
- Note the confirming specialists in the output

**Apply confidence gates:**
- Confidence 7+: show normally in the findings output
- Confidence 5-6: show with caveat "Medium confidence — verify this is actually an issue"
- Confidence 3-4: move to appendix (suppress from main findings)
- Confidence 1-2: suppress entirely

**Compute PR Quality Score:**
After merging, compute the quality score:
`quality_score = max(0, 10 - (critical_count * 2 + informational_count * 0.5))`
Cap at 10. Log this in the review result at the end.

**Output merged findings:**
Present the merged findings in the same format as the current review:

```
SPECIALIST REVIEW: N findings (X critical, Y informational) from Z specialists

[For each finding, in order: CRITICAL first, then INFORMATIONAL, sorted by confidence descending]
[SEVERITY] (confidence: N/10, specialist: name) path:line — summary
  Fix: recommended fix
  [If MULTI-SPECIALIST CONFIRMED: show confirmation note]

PR Quality Score: X/10
```

These findings flow into Step 5 Fix-First alongside the CRITICAL pass findings from Step 4.
The Fix-First heuristic applies identically — specialist findings follow the same AUTO-FIX vs ASK classification.

**Compile per-specialist stats:**
After merging findings, compile a `specialists` object for the review-log entry in Step 5.8.
For each specialist (testing, maintainability, security, performance, data-migration, api-contract, design, red-team):
- If dispatched: `{"dispatched": true, "findings": N, "critical": N, "informational": N}`
- If skipped by scope: `{"dispatched": false, "reason": "scope"}`
- If skipped by gating: `{"dispatched": false, "reason": "gated"}`
- If not applicable (e.g., red-team not activated): omit from the object

Include the Design specialist even though it uses `design-checklist.md` instead of the specialist schema files.
Remember these stats — you will need them for the review-log entry in Step 5.8.

---

### Red Team dispatch (conditional)

**Activation:** Only if DIFF_LINES > 200 OR any specialist produced a CRITICAL finding.

If activated, dispatch one more subagent via the Agent tool (foreground, not background).

The Red Team subagent receives:
1. The red-team checklist from `~/.claude/skills/gstack/review/specialists/red-team.md`
2. The merged specialist findings from Step 4.6 (so it knows what was already caught)
3. The git diff command

Prompt: "You are a red team reviewer. The code has already been reviewed by N specialists
who found the following issues: {merged findings summary}. Your job is to find what they
MISSED. Read the checklist, run `git diff origin/<base>`, and look for gaps.
Output findings as JSON objects (same schema as the specialists). Focus on cross-cutting
concerns, integration boundary issues, and failure modes that specialist checklists
don't cover."

If the Red Team finds additional issues, merge them into the findings list before
Step 5 Fix-First. Red Team findings are tagged with `"specialist":"red-team"`.

If the Red Team returns NO FINDINGS, note: "Red Team review: no additional issues found."
If the Red Team subagent fails or times out, skip silently and continue.

---

## Step 5: Fix-First Review

**Every finding gets action — not just critical ones.**

### Step 5.0: Cross-review finding dedup

Before classifying findings, check if any were previously skipped by the user in a prior review on this branch.

```bash
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-review-read
```

Parse the output: only lines BEFORE `---CONFIG---` are JSONL entries (the output also contains `---CONFIG---` and `---HEAD---` footer sections that are not JSONL — ignore those).

For each JSONL entry that has a `findings` array:
1. Collect all fingerprints where `action: "skipped"`
2. Note the `commit` field from that entry

/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '1,180p' /root/floom-skills/skills/gstack-browse/SKILL.md" in /root/openfeedbacklayer
 succeeded in 0ms:

_LEARN_FILE="${GSTACK_HOME:-$HOME/.gstack}/projects/${SLUG:-unknown}/learnings.jsonl"
if [ -f "$_LEARN_FILE" ]; then
  _LEARN_COUNT=$(wc -l < "$_LEARN_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')
  echo "LEARNINGS: $_LEARN_COUNT entries loaded"
  if [ "$_LEARN_COUNT" -gt 5 ] 2>/dev/null; then
    ~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-learnings-search --limit 3 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
else
  echo "LEARNINGS: 0"
fi
~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-timeline-log '{"skill":"browse","event":"started","branch":"'"$_BRANCH"'","session":"'"$_SESSION_ID"'"}' 2>/dev/null &
_HAS_ROUTING="no"
if [ -f CLAUDE.md ] && grep -q "## Skill routing" CLAUDE.md 2>/dev/null; then
  _HAS_ROUTING="yes"
fi
_ROUTING_DECLINED=$(~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config get routing_declined 2>/dev/null || echo "false")
echo "HAS_ROUTING: $_HAS_ROUTING"
echo "ROUTING_DECLINED: $_ROUTING_DECLINED"
_VENDORED="no"
if [ -d ".claude/skills/gstack" ] && [ ! -L ".claude/skills/gstack" ]; then
  if [ -f ".claude/skills/gstack/VERSION" ] || [ -d ".claude/skills/gstack/.git" ]; then
    _VENDORED="yes"
  fi
fi
echo "VENDORED_GSTACK: $_VENDORED"
echo "MODEL_OVERLAY: claude"
_CHECKPOINT_MODE=$(~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config get checkpoint_mode 2>/dev/null || echo "explicit")
_CHECKPOINT_PUSH=$(~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config get checkpoint_push 2>/dev/null || echo "false")
echo "CHECKPOINT_MODE: $_CHECKPOINT_MODE"
echo "CHECKPOINT_PUSH: $_CHECKPOINT_PUSH"
[ -n "$OPENCLAW_SESSION" ] && echo "SPAWNED_SESSION: true" || true
```

## Plan Mode Safe Operations

In plan mode, allowed because they inform the plan: `$B`, `$D`, `codex exec`/`codex review`, writes to `~/.gstack/`, writes to the plan file, and `open` for generated artifacts.

## Skill Invocation During Plan Mode

If the user invokes a skill in plan mode, the skill takes precedence over generic plan mode behavior. **Treat the skill file as executable instructions, not reference.** Follow it step by step starting from Step 0; the first AskUserQuestion is the workflow entering plan mode, not a violation of it. AskUserQuestion (any variant — `mcp__*__AskUserQuestion` or native; see "AskUserQuestion Format → Tool resolution") satisfies plan mode's end-of-turn requirement. If no variant is callable, fall back to writing the decision brief into the plan file as a `## Decisions to confirm` section + ExitPlanMode — never silently auto-decide. At a STOP point, stop immediately. Do not continue the workflow or call ExitPlanMode there. Commands marked "PLAN MODE EXCEPTION — ALWAYS RUN" execute. Call ExitPlanMode only after the skill workflow completes, or if the user tells you to cancel the skill or leave plan mode.

If `PROACTIVE` is `"false"`, do not auto-invoke or proactively suggest skills. If a skill seems useful, ask: "I think /skillname might help here — want me to run it?"

If `SKILL_PREFIX` is `"true"`, suggest/invoke `/gstack-*` names. Disk paths stay `~/.claude/skills/gstack/[skill-name]/SKILL.md`.

If output shows `UPGRADE_AVAILABLE <old> <new>`: read `~/.claude/skills/gstack/gstack-upgrade/SKILL.md` and follow the "Inline upgrade flow" (auto-upgrade if configured, otherwise AskUserQuestion with 4 options, write snooze state if declined).

If output shows `JUST_UPGRADED <from> <to>`: print "Running gstack v{to} (just updated!)". If `SPAWNED_SESSION` is true, skip feature discovery.

Feature discovery, max one prompt per session:
- Missing `~/.claude/skills/gstack/.feature-prompted-continuous-checkpoint`: AskUserQuestion for Continuous checkpoint auto-commits. If accepted, run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set checkpoint_mode continuous`. Always touch marker.
- Missing `~/.claude/skills/gstack/.feature-prompted-model-overlay`: inform "Model overlays are active. MODEL_OVERLAY shows the patch." Always touch marker.

After upgrade prompts, continue workflow.

If `WRITING_STYLE_PENDING` is `yes`: ask once about writing style:

> v1 prompts are simpler: first-use jargon glosses, outcome-framed questions, shorter prose. Keep default or restore terse?

Options:
- A) Keep the new default (recommended — good writing helps everyone)
- B) Restore V0 prose — set `explain_level: terse`

If A: leave `explain_level` unset (defaults to `default`).
If B: run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set explain_level terse`.

Always run (regardless of choice):
```bash
rm -f ~/.gstack/.writing-style-prompt-pending
touch ~/.gstack/.writing-style-prompted
```

Skip if `WRITING_STYLE_PENDING` is `no`.

If `LAKE_INTRO` is `no`: say "gstack follows the **Boil the Lake** principle — do the complete thing when AI makes marginal cost near-zero. Read more: https://garryslist.org/posts/boil-the-ocean" Offer to open:

```bash
open https://garryslist.org/posts/boil-the-ocean
touch ~/.gstack/.completeness-intro-seen
```

Only run `open` if yes. Always run `touch`.

If `TEL_PROMPTED` is `no` AND `LAKE_INTRO` is `yes`: ask telemetry once via AskUserQuestion:

> Help gstack get better. Share usage data only: skill, duration, crashes, stable device ID. No code, file paths, or repo names.

Options:
- A) Help gstack get better! (recommended)
- B) No thanks

If A: run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set telemetry community`

If B: ask follow-up:

> Anonymous mode sends only aggregate usage, no unique ID.

Options:
- A) Sure, anonymous is fine
- B) No thanks, fully off

If B→A: run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set telemetry anonymous`
If B→B: run `~/.claude/skills/gstack/bin/gstack-config set telemetry off`

Always run:
```bash
touch ~/.gstack/.telemetry-prompted
```

   160	        })

   162	      if (!uploadError && uploadData) {
   163	        const { data: urlData } = deps.supabase
   164	          .storage
   165	          .from('feedback')
   166	          .getPublicUrl(filename)
   167	        screenshotUrl = urlData.publicUrl
   168	      }
   169	
   170	      screenshotBase64 = `data:image/png;base64,${buffer.toString('base64')}`
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    let aiData: FeedbackAIData | null = null
   174	    const geminiKey = deps.env.GEMINI_API_KEY
   175	    if (geminiKey) {
   176	      aiData = await deps.analyze({
   177	        messageRaw,
   178	        screenshotBase64: screenshotBase64 || undefined,
   179	        geminiApiKey: geminiKey,
   180	      })
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    const { data: feedback, error } = await deps.supabase
   184	      .from('feedback')
   185	      .insert({
   186	        page_url: pageUrl,
   187	        user_agent: userAgent,
   188	        message_raw: messageRaw,
   189	        screenshot_url: screenshotUrl,
   190	        ai_data: aiData,
   191	        project_id: projectId,
   192	        status: 'new',
   193	        submitter_email: submitterEmail,
   194	        subscribe,
   195	        journey_stage: 'received',
   196	      })
   197	      .select('id')
   198	      .single()
   199	
   200	    if (error || !feedback) {
   201	      console.error('[Feedback] Database error:', error)
   202	      return json({ error: 'Failed to save feedback' }, { status: 500 })
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    let githubIssue: { number: number; url: string } | null = null
   206	    const githubToken = deps.env.GITHUB_TOKEN
   207	    const githubRepo = deps.env.GITHUB_FEEDBACK_REPO
   208	
   209	    if (githubToken && githubRepo) {
   210	      githubIssue = await deps.createIssue({
   211	        token: githubToken,
   212	        repo: githubRepo,
   213	        aiData,
   214	        messageRaw,
   215	        pageUrl,
   216	        screenshotUrl,
   217	      })
   218	
   219	      if (githubIssue) {
   220	        const { error: updateError } = await deps.supabase
   221	          .from('feedback')
   222	          .update({
   223	            github_issue_number: githubIssue.number,
   224	            github_issue_url: githubIssue.url,
   225	            github_repo: githubRepo,
   226	          })
   227	          .eq('id', feedback.id)
   228	
   229	        if (updateError) {
   230	          console.error('[Feedback] GitHub issue update error:', updateError)
   231	        }
   232	      }
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    const resendKey = deps.env.RESEND_API_KEY
   236	    const resendFrom = deps.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'feedback@yourdomain.com'
   237	
   238	    if (submitterEmail && subscribe && resendKey && githubIssue) {
   239	      deps.sendConfirmation({
   240	        resendKey,
   241	        from: resendFrom,
   242	        to: submitterEmail,
   243	        issueNumber: githubIssue.number,
   244	        issueUrl: githubIssue.url,
   245	        title: aiData?.title || messageRaw.slice(0, 60),
   246	      })
   247	    }
   248	
   249	    sendNotificationEmail(deps.env, {
   250	      id: feedback.id,
   251	      message_raw: messageRaw,
   252	      page_url: pageUrl,
   253	      ai_data: aiData,
   254	    })
   255	
   256	    return json({
   257	      id: feedback.id,
   258	      ai_data: aiData,
   259	      message: 'Feedback received',
   260	    })
   261	  } catch (error) {
   262	    console.error('[Feedback] Error:', error)
   263	    return json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, { status: 500 })
   264	  }
   265	}

     1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'

     8	import type { ServerEnv } from './feedback-core'
     9	
    10	export interface WebhookDeps {
    11	  supabase: any
    12	  env: ServerEnv
    13	  sendStage: typeof sendStageEmail
    14	}
    15	
    16	let supabaseAdmin: ReturnType<typeof createClient> | null = null
    17	
    18	function getSupabaseAdmin(env: ServerEnv) {
    19	  if (!supabaseAdmin) {
    20	    supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    21	      env.SUPABASE_URL!,
    22	      env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!
    23	    )
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  return supabaseAdmin
    27	}
    28	
    29	export function realDeps(env: ServerEnv = process.env): WebhookDeps {
    30	  return {
    31	    supabase: getSupabaseAdmin(env),
    32	    env,
    33	    sendStage: sendStageEmail,
    34	  }
    35	}
    36	
    37	const STAGE_RANK: Record<JourneyStage, number> = {
    38	  received: 0,
    39	  triaged: 1,
    40	  in_progress: 2,
    41	  shipped: 3,
    42	  wont_fix: 3,
    43	}
    44	
    45	function json(body: unknown, init?: ResponseInit): Response {
    46	  return Response.json(body, init)
    47	}
    48	
    49	export async function handleWebhook(request: Request, deps: WebhookDeps): Promise<Response> {
    50	  const raw = await request.text()
    51	  const secret = deps.env.GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET
    52	
    53	  if (!secret || !verifyGitHubSignature(secret, raw, request.headers.get('x-hub-signature-256'))) {
    54	    return json({ error: 'Invalid signature' }, { status: 401 })
    55	  }
    56	
    57	  try {
    58	    if (request.headers.get('x-github-event') !== 'issues') {
    59	      return json({ ignored: true })
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    const payload = JSON.parse(raw)
    63	    const stage = stageFromGitHubEvent(payload)
    64	
    65	    if (!stage) {
    66	      return json({ ignored: true })
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    const repoFullName = payload.repository?.full_name
    70	    if (!repoFullName) {
    71	      return json({ ignored: true })
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    if (deps.env.GITHUB_FEEDBACK_REPO && repoFullName !== deps.env.GITHUB_FEEDBACK_REPO) {
    75	      return json({ ignored: true })
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    const { data: feedback, error } = await deps.supabase
    79	      .from('feedback')
    80	      .select('id, submitter_email, subscribe, github_issue_url, journey_stage, last_emailed_stage, ai_data')
    81	      .eq('github_repo', repoFullName)
    82	      .eq('github_issue_number', payload.issue.number)
    83	      .maybeSingle()
    84	
    85	    if (error) {
    86	      throw error
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    if (!feedback || !feedback.subscribe || !feedback.submitter_email) {
    90	      return json({ ignored: true })
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    if (feedback.last_emailed_stage === stage) {
    94	      return json({ duplicate: true })
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    const currentStage = (feedback.journey_stage || 'received') as JourneyStage
    98	    if (STAGE_RANK[stage] <= STAGE_RANK[currentStage]) {
    99	      return json({ ignored: true })
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    const resendKey = deps.env.RESEND_API_KEY
   103	    if (!resendKey) {
   104	      return json({ ignored: true })
   105	    }
   106	
   107	    // Atomic single-statement claim via RPC. A prior approach used
   108	    // .update().or(last_emailed_stage.is.null,...) but PostgREST rejects an
   109	    // `or` filter on a mutation (HTTP 400, SQLSTATE 42703), so it never
   110	    // advanced the stage against real Supabase. The function uses
   111	    // `IS DISTINCT FROM` so NULL and non-null are handled in one atomic UPDATE.
   112	    const { data: claim, error: claimError } = await deps.supabase
   113	      .rpc('claim_feedback_stage', { p_id: feedback.id, p_stage: stage })
   114	
   115	    if (claimError) {
   116	      throw claimError
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    if (!claim || claim.length !== 1) {
   120	      return json({ duplicate: true })
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    await deps.sendStage({
   124	      resendKey,
   125	      from: deps.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'feedback@yourdomain.com',
   126	      to: feedback.submitter_email,
   127	      stage,
   128	      issueNumber: payload.issue.number,
   129	      issueUrl: feedback.github_issue_url || payload.issue.html_url,
   130	      title: feedback.ai_data?.title || payload.issue.title,
   131	    })
   132	
   133	    return json({ ok: true })
   134	  } catch (error) {
   135	    console.error('[OpenFeedbackLayer] GitHub webhook error:', error)
   136	    return json({ ok: true })
   137	  }
   138	}

    93	function sanitizeUrl(value: string): string {

   107	    url.password = ''
   108	    url.search = ''
   109	    url.hash = ''

   111	    if (hasUnsafePathToken(url.pathname)) {

   115	    // Clean URL (origin + path only). URL autolink defanging happens later

   209	function safePageUrl(pageUrl: string): string {
   210	  return redactSensitive(sanitizeUrl(pageUrl))
   211	}
   212	
   213	function markdownList(items: string[]): string {
   214	  return items.map((item) => `- ${redactSensitive(item)}`).join('\n')
   215	}
   216	
   217	function buildIssueBody(params: CreateFeedbackIssueParams): string {
   218	  const { aiData, messageRaw, pageUrl, screenshotUrl } = params
   219	  const body = [
   220	    '## Summary',
   221	    redactSensitive(aiData?.short_summary || 'New feedback received.'),
   222	    '',
   223	    '## Original message',
   224	    `> ${redactSensitive(messageRaw).replace(/\n/g, '\n> ')}`,
   225	    '',
   226	    '## Key details',
   227	    aiData?.key_details?.length ? markdownList(aiData.key_details) : '- None provided',
   228	  ]
   229	
   230	  if (aiData?.steps?.length) {
   231	    body.push('', '## Steps', markdownList(aiData.steps))
   232	  }
   233	
   234	  if (aiData?.expected) {
   235	    body.push('', '## Expected', redactSensitive(aiData.expected))
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  body.push('', '## Page URL', safePageUrl(pageUrl))
   239	
   240	  if (screenshotUrl) {
   241	    const safeScreenshotUrl = sanitizeUrl(screenshotUrl)
   242	    body.push(
   243	      '',
   244	      '## Screenshot',
   245	      safeScreenshotUrl === UNSAFE_URL
   246	        ? UNSAFE_URL
   247	        : `![Feedback screenshot](${encodeMarkdownUnsafeUrl(safeScreenshotUrl)})`,
   248	    )
   249	  }
   250	
   251	  body.push('', '_Filed via OpenFeedbackLayer_')
   252	
   253	  return body.join('\n')
   254	}
   255	
   256	export function buildFeedbackIssuePayload(params: CreateFeedbackIssueParams): FeedbackIssuePayload {
   257	  const [owner, name] = params.repo.split('/')
   258	  if (!owner || !name) {
   259	    throw new Error('GITHUB_FEEDBACK_REPO must use owner/name format')
   260	  }
   261	
   262	  const fallbackTitle = redactSensitive(params.messageRaw).slice(0, 60) || 'New feedback'
   263	  const title = redactSensitive(params.aiData?.title || fallbackTitle)
   264	  const labels = [
   265	    'feedback',
   266	    `category:${params.aiData?.suggested_category || 'other'}`,
   267	    `priority:${params.aiData?.suggested_priority || 'medium'}`,
   268	  ]
   269	
   270	  return {
   271	    owner,
   272	    repo: name,
   273	    title,
   274	    body: buildIssueBody(params),
   275	    labels,
   276	  }
   277	}
   278	
   279	export async function createFeedbackIssue({
   280	  token,
   281	  repo,
   282	  aiData,
   283	  messageRaw,
   284	  pageUrl,
   285	  screenshotUrl,
   286	}: CreateFeedbackIssueParams): Promise<{ number: number; url: string } | null> {
   287	  try {
   288	    const issuePayload = buildFeedbackIssuePayload({ token, repo, aiData, messageRaw, pageUrl, screenshotUrl })
   289	
   290	    const { Octokit } = await import('@octokit/rest')
   291	    const octokit = new Octokit({ auth: token })
   292	
   293	    const issue = await octokit.rest.issues.create({
   294	      owner: issuePayload.owner,
   295	      repo: issuePayload.repo,
   296	      title: issuePayload.title,
   297	      body: issuePayload.body,
   298	      labels: issuePayload.labels,
   299	    })
   300	
   301	    return {
   302	      number: issue.data.number,
   303	      url: issue.data.html_url,
   304	    }
   305	  } catch (error) {
   306	    console.error('[OpenFeedbackLayer] Failed to create GitHub issue:', error)
   307	    return null
   308	  }
   309	}
   310	
   311	export function stageFromGitHubEvent(event: {
   312	  action: string
   313	  label?: { name: string }
   314	  issue: { state: string; state_reason?: string | null }
   315	}): JourneyStage | null {
   316	  if (event.action === 'closed') {
   317	    return event.issue.state_reason === 'not_planned' ? 'wont_fix' : 'shipped'
   318	  }
   319	
   320	  if (event.action === 'labeled') {
   321	    const label = event.label?.name.toLowerCase().trim()
   322	    if (label === 'triaged') return 'triaged'
   323	    if (label === 'in progress' || label === 'in-progress' || label === 'wip') {
   324	      return 'in_progress'
   325	    }
   326	  }
   327	
   328	  return null
   329	}
   330	
   331	export function verifyGitHubSignature(
   332	  secret: string,
   333	  rawBody: string,
   334	  signatureHeader: string | null
   335	): boolean {
   336	  if (!secret || !signatureHeader?.startsWith('sha256=')) return false
   337	
   338	  const signature = signatureHeader.slice('sha256='.length)
   339	  if (!/^[a-f0-9]+$/i.test(signature)) return false
   340	
   341	  const expected = Buffer.from(createHmac('sha256', secret).update(rawBody).digest('hex'), 'hex')
   342	  const received = Buffer.from(signature, 'hex')
   343	
   344	  if (expected.length !== received.length) return false
   345	
   346	  return timingSafeEqual(expected, received)
   347	}

     1	/**
     2	 * OpenFeedbackLayer API Route Template
     3	 * Copy this file to your Next.js app: app/api/feedback/route.ts
     4	 *
     5	 * Required environment variables:
     6	 * - GEMINI_API_KEY: For AI classification
     7	 * - SUPABASE_URL: Your Supabase project URL
     8	 * - SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY: For server-side DB operations
     9	 *
    10	 * Optional:
    11	 * - RESEND_API_KEY: For email notifications
    12	 * - FEEDBACK_NOTIFY_EMAIL: Email to notify on new feedback
    13	 */
    14	
    15	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
    16	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
    17	import { analyzeFeedback } from 'openfeedbacklayer'
    18	
    19	// Initialize Supabase admin client
    20	const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
    21	  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,
    22	  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!
    23	)
    24	
    25	// Rate limiting (in-memory for simplicity)
    26	const rateLimitMap = new Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }>()
    27	const RATE_LIMIT = 10
    28	const RATE_WINDOW = 60 * 1000 // 1 minute
    29	
    30	function checkRateLimit(ip: string): boolean {
    31	  const now = Date.now()
    32	  const entry = rateLimitMap.get(ip)
    33	
    34	  if (!entry || now > entry.resetAt) {
    35	    rateLimitMap.set(ip, { count: 1, resetAt: now + RATE_WINDOW })
    36	    return true
    37	  }
    38	
    39	  if (entry.count >= RATE_LIMIT) {
    40	    return false
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  entry.count++
    44	  return true
    45	}
    46	
    47	// Optional: Send notification email
    48	async function sendNotificationEmail(feedback: {
    49	  id: string
    50	  message_raw: string
    51	  page_url: string
    52	  ai_data: { suggested_category?: string; suggested_priority?: string } | null
    53	}) {
    54	  const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY
    55	  const notifyEmail = process.env.FEEDBACK_NOTIFY_EMAIL
    56	
    57	  if (!resendKey || !notifyEmail) return
    58	
    59	  try {
    60	    const { Resend } = await import('resend')
    61	    const resend = new Resend(resendKey)
    62	
    63	    const category = feedback.ai_data?.suggested_category || 'unknown'
    64	    const priority = feedback.ai_data?.suggested_priority || 'medium'
    65	    const emoji = priority === 'high' ? '🔴' : priority === 'medium' ? '🟡' : '🟢'
    66	
    67	    await resend.emails.send({
    68	      from: process.env.RESEND_FROM_EMAIL || 'feedback@yourdomain.com',
    69	      to: notifyEmail,
    70	      subject: `${emoji} New Feedback: ${category}`,
    71	      html: `
    72	        <h2>New Feedback Received</h2>
    73	        <p><strong>Category:</strong> ${category}</p>
    74	        <p><strong>Priority:</strong> ${priority}</p>
    75	        <p><strong>Page:</strong> ${feedback.page_url}</p>
    76	        <hr>
    77	        <p><strong>Message:</strong></p>
    78	        <blockquote>${feedback.message_raw.replace(/\n/g, '<br>')}</blockquote>
    79	      `,
    80	    })
    81	  } catch (error) {
    82	    console.error('[Feedback] Failed to send email:', error)
    83	  }
    84	}
    85	
    86	export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    87	  try {
    88	    // Rate limiting
    89	    const ip = request.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0] ||
    90	               request.headers.get('x-real-ip') || 'unknown'
    91	
    92	    if (!checkRateLimit(ip)) {
    93	      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Too many requests' }, { status: 429 })
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    const formData = await request.formData()
    97	
    98	    // HONEYPOT: If this field has value, it's a bot
    99	    const honeypot = formData.get('website') as string
   100	    if (honeypot && honeypot.trim().length > 0) {
   101	      console.log('[Feedback] Bot detected via honeypot')
   102	      return NextResponse.json({ id: 'fake-id', message: 'Feedback sent' })
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    const messageRaw = formData.get('message') as string
   106	    const screenshot = formData.get('screenshot') as File | null
   107	    const projectId = (formData.get('project') as string) || null
   108	
   109	    if (!messageRaw?.trim()) {
   110	      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Message is required' }, { status: 400 })
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    const pageUrl = request.headers.get('x-page-url') || request.headers.get('referer') || 'unknown'
   114	    const userAgent = request.headers.get('user-agent') || null
   115	
   116	    // Handle screenshot upload to Supabase Storage
   117	    let screenshotUrl: string | null = null
   118	    let screenshotBase64: string | null = null
   119	
   120	    if (screenshot && screenshot.size > 0) {
   121	      const buffer = Buffer.from(await screenshot.arrayBuffer())
   122	      const filename = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(7)}.png`
   123	
   124	      const { data: uploadData, error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin
   125	        .storage
   126	        .from('feedback')
   127	        .upload(filename, buffer, {
   128	          contentType: 'image/png',
   129	          cacheControl: '3600',
   130	        })
   131	
   132	      if (!uploadError && uploadData) {
   133	        const { data: urlData } = supabaseAdmin
   134	          .storage
   135	          .from('feedback')
   136	          .getPublicUrl(filename)
   137	        screenshotUrl = urlData.publicUrl
   138	      }
   139	
   140	      screenshotBase64 = `data:image/png;base64,${buffer.toString('base64')}`
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    // AI classification
   144	    let aiData = null
   145	    const geminiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY
   146	    if (geminiKey) {
   147	      aiData = await analyzeFeedback({
   148	        messageRaw,
   149	        screenshotBase64: screenshotBase64 || undefined,
   150	        geminiApiKey: geminiKey,
   151	      })
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    // Store feedback
   155	    const { data: feedback, error } = await supabaseAdmin
   156	      .from('feedback')
   157	      .insert({
   158	        page_url: pageUrl,
   159	        user_agent: userAgent,
   160	        message_raw: messageRaw,
   161	        screenshot_url: screenshotUrl,
   162	        ai_data: aiData,
   163	        project_id: projectId,
   164	        status: 'new',
   165	      })
   166	      .select('id')
   167	      .single()
   168	
   169	    if (error) {
   170	      console.error('[Feedback] Database error:', error)
   171	      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to save feedback' }, { status: 500 })
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    // Send email notification (async)
   175	    sendNotificationEmail({
   176	      id: feedback.id,
   177	      message_raw: messageRaw,
   178	      page_url: pageUrl,
   179	      ai_data: aiData,
   180	    })
   181	
   182	    return NextResponse.json({
   183	      id: feedback.id,
   184	      ai_data: aiData,
   185	      message: 'Feedback received',
   186	    })
   187	  } catch (error) {
   188	    console.error('[Feedback] Error:', error)
   189	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, { status: 500 })
   190	  }
   191	}

     3	/**
     4	 * OpenFeedbackLayer Widget
     5	 * A floating feedback button with AI classification
     6	 */
     7	
     8	import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
     9	import type { FeedbackWidgetProps, FeedbackData } from '../lib/types'
    10	
    11	const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
    12	  primary: '#2563eb', // blue-600
    13	  primaryHover: '#1d4ed8', // blue-700
    14	  success: '#16a34a', // green-600
    15	  text: '#1f2937', // gray-800
    16	  textMuted: '#6b7280', // gray-500
    17	  bg: '#ffffff',
    18	  border: '#e5e7eb', // gray-200
    19	}
    20	
    21	export function FeedbackWidget({
    22	  apiEndpoint = '/api/feedback',
    23	  projectId,
    24	  position = 'bottom-right',
    25	  primaryColor = DEFAULT_COLORS.primary,
    26	  buttonText = 'Feedback',
    27	  placeholder = 'Describe your feedback, bug, or feature request...',
    28	  collectEmail = true,
    29	  emailPlaceholder = 'Your email (optional, to hear back)',
    30	  onSubmit,
    31	  onError,
    32	}: FeedbackWidgetProps) {
    33	  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false)
    34	  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
    35	  const [screenshotFile, setScreenshotFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
    36	  const [screenshotPreview, setScreenshotPreview] = useState<string | null>(null)
    37	  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false)
    38	  const [isSent, setIsSent] = useState(false)
    39	  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
    40	  const [subscribe, setSubscribe] = useState(false)
    41	  const [aiResponse, setAiResponse] = useState<string | null>(null)
    42	  const [classification, setClassification] = useState<{
    43	    category: string
    44	    feature_area: string
    45	    priority: string
    46	  } | null>(null)
    47	  const [honeypot, setHoneypot] = useState('')
    48	
    49	  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
    50	  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null)
    51	
    52	  // Position styles
    53	  const positionStyles: Record<string, React.CSSProperties> = {
    54	    'bottom-right': { bottom: 20, right: 20 },
    55	    'bottom-left': { bottom: 20, left: 20 },
    56	    'top-right': { top: 20, right: 20 },
    57	    'top-left': { top: 20, left: 20 },
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  // Reset state when closing
    61	  const handleClose = () => {
    62	    setIsOpen(false)
    63	    if (isSent) {
    64	      // Reset after closing sent state
    65	      setTimeout(() => {
    66	        setMessage('')
    67	        setScreenshotFile(null)
    68	        setScreenshotPreview(null)
    69	        setEmail('')
    70	        setSubscribe(false)
    71	        setIsSent(false)
    72	        setAiResponse(null)
    73	        setClassification(null)
    74	      }, 300)
    75	    }
    76	  }
    77	
    78	  // Handle paste for screenshots
    79	  useEffect(() => {
    80	    const handlePaste = (e: ClipboardEvent) => {
    81	      if (!isOpen) return
    82	      const items = e.clipboardData?.items
    83	      if (!items) return
    84	
    85	      for (const item of items) {
    86	        if (item.type.startsWith('image/')) {
    87	          const file = item.getAsFile()
    88	          if (file) {
    89	            handleScreenshotSelect(file)
    90	          }
    91	        }
    92	      }
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    document.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste)
    96	    return () => document.removeEventListener('paste', handlePaste)
    97	  }, [isOpen])
    98	
    99	  // Handle screenshot selection
   100	  const handleScreenshotSelect = (file: File) => {
   101	    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
   102	      alert('Image must be less than 5MB')
   103	      return
   104	    }
   105	    setScreenshotFile(file)
   106	    const reader = new FileReader()
   107	    reader.onloadend = () => setScreenshotPreview(reader.result as string)
   108	    reader.readAsDataURL(file)
   109	  }
   110	
   111	  // Handle drag & drop
   112	  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
   113	    e.preventDefault()
   114	    const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0]
   115	    if (file?.type.startsWith('image/')) {
   116	      handleScreenshotSelect(file)
   117	    }
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  // Format priority for display
   121	  const formatPriority = (priority: string) => {
   122	    const map: Record<string, string> = { high: 'P0', medium: 'P1', low: 'P2' }
   123	    return map[priority] || 'P1'
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  // Send feedback
   127	  const handleSend = async () => {
   128	    if (!message.trim() || isSending) return
   129	
   130	    setIsSending(true)
   131	    const trimmedEmail = email.trim()
   132	
   133	    try {
   134	      const formData = new FormData()
   135	      formData.append('message', message)
   136	      formData.append('website', honeypot) // Honeypot field
   137	      formData.append('subscribe', subscribe && trimmedEmail ? 'true' : 'false')
   138	
   139	      if (trimmedEmail) {
   140	        formData.append('email', trimmedEmail)
   141	      }
   142	
   143	      if (projectId) {
   144	        formData.append('project', projectId)
   145	      }
   146	
   147	      if (screenshotFile) {
   148	        formData.append('screenshot', screenshotFile)
   149	      }
   150	
   151	      const response = await fetch(apiEndpoint, {
   152	        method: 'POST',
   153	        headers: {
   154	          'x-page-url': typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : '',
   155	        },
   156	        body: formData,
   157	      })
   158	
   159	      if (!response.ok) {
   160	        throw new Error('Failed to send feedback')
   161	      }
   162	
   163	      const result = await response.json()
   164	      const aiData = result.ai_data
   165	
   166	      if (aiData) {
   167	        setClassification({
   168	          category: aiData.suggested_category || 'other',
   169	          feature_area: aiData.suggested_feature_area || 'general',
   170	          priority: formatPriority(aiData.suggested_priority || 'medium'),
   171	        })
   172	        setAiResponse(aiData.short_summary || 'Thank you for your feedback!')
   173	      } else {
   174	        setClassification({
   175	          category: 'feedback',
   176	          feature_area: 'general',
   177	          priority: 'P1',
   178	        })
   179	        setAiResponse('Thank you for your feedback!')
   180	      }
   181	
   182	      setIsSent(true)
   183	
   184	      // Callback
   185	      if (onSubmit) {
   186	        onSubmit({
   187	          message_raw: message,
   188	          page_url: typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : '',
   189	          ai_data: aiData,
   190	          submitter_email: trimmedEmail || null,
   191	          subscribe: Boolean(subscribe && trimmedEmail),
   192	        })
   193	      }
   194	    } catch (error) {
   195	      console.error('[OpenFeedbackLayer] Send error:', error)
   196	      setClassification({
   197	        category: 'feedback',
   198	        feature_area: 'general',
   199	        priority: 'P1',
   200	      })
   201	      setAiResponse('Thank you for your feedback!')
   202	      setIsSent(true)
   203	
   204	      if (onError && error instanceof Error) {
   205	        onError(error)
   206	      }
   207	    } finally {
   208	      setIsSending(false)
   209	    }
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  // Styles
   213	  const styles = {
   214	    container: {
   215	      position: 'fixed' as const,

     1	-- OpenFeedbackLayer Database Schema
     2	-- Run this migration in your Supabase SQL Editor
     3	
     4	-- Create feedback table
     5	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS feedback (
     6	  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
     7	
     8	  -- Source information
     9	  page_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    10	  user_agent TEXT,
    11	  project_id TEXT,
    12	
    13	  -- User input
    14	  message_raw TEXT NOT NULL,
    15	  screenshot_url TEXT,
    16	
    17	  -- AI classification (JSONB for flexibility)
    18	  ai_data JSONB,
    19	
    20	  -- Status tracking
    21	  status TEXT DEFAULT 'new' CHECK (status IN ('new', 'in_progress', 'resolved', 'closed')),
    22	
    23	  -- Optional feedback journey and GitHub issue tracking
    24	  submitter_email TEXT,
    25	  subscribe BOOLEAN DEFAULT false NOT NULL,
    26	  github_issue_number INTEGER,
    27	  github_issue_url TEXT,
    28	  github_repo TEXT,
    29	  journey_stage TEXT DEFAULT 'received' NOT NULL
    30	    CHECK (journey_stage IN ('received','triaged','in_progress','shipped','wont_fix')),
    31	  last_emailed_stage TEXT,
    32	
    33	  -- Optional: link to your users table
    34	  -- user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    35	
    36	  -- Timestamps
    37	  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    38	  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
    39	);
    40	
    41	-- Indexes for common queries
    42	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_status ON feedback(status);
    43	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_created_at ON feedback(created_at DESC);
    44	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_project_id ON feedback(project_id) WHERE project_id IS NOT NULL;
    45	
    46	-- AI classification indexes (for filtering by category/priority)
    47	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_category ON feedback((ai_data->>'suggested_category'));
    48	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_priority ON feedback((ai_data->>'suggested_priority'));
    49	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_feature_area ON feedback((ai_data->>'suggested_feature_area'));
    50	CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_feedback_repo_issue
    51	  ON feedback(github_repo, github_issue_number)
    52	  WHERE github_issue_number IS NOT NULL;
    53	
    54	-- Auto-update updated_at
    55	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_feedback_updated_at()
    56	RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
    57	BEGIN
    58	  NEW.updated_at = now();
    59	  RETURN NEW;
    60	END;
    61	$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
    62	
    63	CREATE TRIGGER feedback_updated_at
    64	  BEFORE UPDATE ON feedback

    87	-- OpenFeedbackLayer Feedback Journey
    88	-- Adds optional submitter updates and GitHub issue tracking.
    89	
    90	ALTER TABLE feedback
    91	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS submitter_email TEXT,
    92	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS subscribe BOOLEAN DEFAULT false NOT NULL,
    93	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS github_issue_number INTEGER,

   103	  ON feedback(github_repo, github_issue_number)
   104	  WHERE github_issue_number IS NOT NULL;
   105	
   106	COMMENT ON COLUMN feedback.journey_stage IS 'OpenFeedbackLayer feedback journey stage for GitHub issue and subscriber update flow';
   107	-- OpenFeedbackLayer: OPTIONAL screenshot storage
   108	--
   109	-- Run this ONLY if you want screenshot attachments. It requires Supabase
   110	-- Storage (the `storage` schema). The core feedback table (001) and the
   111	-- journey columns (002) do NOT depend on this; skip this file if you do not
   112	-- use screenshots or are not on Supabase.
   113	
   114	-- Create storage bucket for screenshots
   115	INSERT INTO storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit, allowed_mime_types)
   116	VALUES (
   117	  'feedback',
   118	  'feedback',
   119	  true,
   120	  5242880,  -- 5MB max
   121	  ARRAY['image/png', 'image/jpeg', 'image/gif', 'image/webp']
   122	)
   123	ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;
   124	
   125	-- Storage policy: allow public uploads
   126	CREATE POLICY "Allow public uploads to feedback bucket"
   127	  ON storage.objects FOR INSERT
   128	  WITH CHECK (bucket_id = 'feedback');
   129	
   130	-- Storage policy: allow public reads
   131	CREATE POLICY "Allow public reads from feedback bucket"
   132	  ON storage.objects FOR SELECT
   133	  USING (bucket_id = 'feedback');
   134	-- OpenFeedbackLayer: atomic, forward-only stage-claim for the webhook journey.
   135	--
   136	-- The webhook must advance journey_stage forward-only and emit exactly one
   137	-- subscriber email per stage even under concurrent / duplicate / out-of-order
   138	-- GitHub deliveries. A check-then-write in application code races; a PostgREST
   139	-- `or` filter on a mutation is rejected (SQLSTATE 42703). This function does
   140	-- the whole decision in one atomic statement:
   141	--   * last_emailed_stage IS DISTINCT FROM p_stage  -> not already emailed
   142	--     (NULL-safe: a never-emailed row is claimable)
   143	--   * rank(p_stage) > rank(current journey_stage)   -> strictly forward only,
   144	--     so a late 'triaged' cannot regress a row already at 'shipped'
   145	-- It returns the row id only to the caller that won the claim; concurrent or
   146	-- duplicate or backward deliveries get zero rows and send no email.
   147	
   148	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION ofl_stage_rank(p_stage text)
   149	RETURNS int
   150	LANGUAGE sql
   151	IMMUTABLE
   152	AS $$
   153	  SELECT CASE p_stage
   154	    WHEN 'received'    THEN 0
   155	    WHEN 'triaged'     THEN 1
   156	    WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 2
   157	    WHEN 'shipped'     THEN 3
   158	    WHEN 'wont_fix'    THEN 3
   159	    ELSE -1
   160	  END;
   161	$$;
   162	
   163	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION claim_feedback_stage(p_id uuid, p_stage text)
   164	RETURNS TABLE (id uuid)
   165	LANGUAGE sql
   166	AS $$
   167	  UPDATE feedback
   168	     SET journey_stage = p_stage,
   169	         last_emailed_stage = p_stage
   170	   WHERE feedback.id = p_id
   171	     AND feedback.last_emailed_stage IS DISTINCT FROM p_stage
   172	     AND ofl_stage_rank(p_stage)
   173	         > ofl_stage_rank(COALESCE(feedback.journey_stage, 'received'))
   174	  RETURNING feedback.id;
   175	$$;
   176	
   177	COMMENT ON FUNCTION claim_feedback_stage(uuid, text) IS
   178	  'OpenFeedbackLayer: atomic forward-only claim of a feedback journey stage; returns id only to the winning caller.';
   179	
   180	-- Server-only. Supabase grants EXECUTE on new functions to PUBLIC (which
   181	-- includes anon and authenticated) by default; revoke that and grant only the
   182	-- service_role used by the server-side webhook handler, so the public REST
   183	-- API cannot drive journey state.
   184	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION ofl_stage_rank(text) FROM PUBLIC;
   185	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION claim_feedback_stage(uuid, text) FROM PUBLIC;
   186	DO $$
   187	BEGIN
   188	  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'anon') THEN
   189	    EXECUTE 'REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION ofl_stage_rank(text) FROM anon';
   190	    EXECUTE 'REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION claim_feedback_stage(uuid, text) FROM anon';
   191	  END IF;
   192	  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'authenticated') THEN
   193	    EXECUTE 'REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION ofl_stage_rank(text) FROM authenticated';
   194	    EXECUTE 'REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION claim_feedback_stage(uuid, text) FROM authenticated';
   195	  END IF;
   196	  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'service_role') THEN
   197	    EXECUTE 'GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION claim_feedback_stage(uuid, text) TO service_role';
   198	  END IF;
   199	END $$;

   435	                        ...styles.badge,
   436	                        backgroundColor: '#f3e8ff',
   437	                        color: '#7c3aed',
   438	                      }}
   439	                    >
   440	                      {classification.feature_area}
   441	                    </span>
   442	                    <span
   443	                      style={{

   449	                            ? '#fef3c7'
   450	                            : '#dcfce7',
   451	                        color:

   453	                            ? '#dc2626'
   454	                            : classification.priority === 'P1'

   457	                      }}
   458	                    >
   459	                      {classification.priority}
   460	                    </span>

   463	                <button

   465	                  onClick={handleClose}
   466	                >
   467	                  Close
   468	                </button>
   469	              </div>
   470	            ) : (
   471	              // Input state
   472	              <>
   473	                {/* Honeypot - hidden from users */}
   474	                <input
   475	                  type="text"
   476	                  name="website"
   477	                  value={honeypot}
   478	                  onChange={(e) => setHoneypot(e.target.value)}
   479	                  tabIndex={-1}
   480	                  autoComplete="off"
   481	                  style={{
   482	                    position: 'absolute',
   483	                    left: '-9999px',
   484	                    opacity: 0,
   485	                    height: 0,
   486	                    width: 0,
   487	                    pointerEvents: 'none',
   488	                  }}
   489	                  aria-hidden="true"
   490	                />
   491	
   492	                <div
   493	                  onDrop={handleDrop}
   494	                  onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
   495	                >
   496	                  <textarea
   497	                    ref={textareaRef}
   498	                    style={styles.textarea}
   499	                    value={message}
   500	                    onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
   501	                    placeholder={placeholder}
   502	                    autoFocus
   503	                  />
   504	                </div>
   505	
   506	                {collectEmail && (
   507	                  <>
   508	                    <input
   509	                      type="email"
   510	                      style={styles.emailInput}
   511	                      value={email}
   512	                      onChange={(e) => {
   513	                        const wasEmpty = email.trim().length === 0
   514	                        const nextEmail = e.target.value
   515	                        setEmail(nextEmail)

   517	                        if (!nextEmail.trim()) {
   518	                          setSubscribe(false)
   519	                        } else if (wasEmpty) {
   520	                          setSubscribe(true)
   521	                        }
   522	                      }}
   523	                      placeholder={emailPlaceholder}
   524	                    />
   525	                    <label style={styles.subscribeRow}>
   526	                      <input
   527	                        type="checkbox"
   528	                        style={styles.checkbox}
   529	                        checked={Boolean(email.trim() && subscribe)}
   530	                        disabled={!email.trim()}
   531	                        onChange={(e) => setSubscribe(e.target.checked)}
   532	                      />
   533	                      Email me when this is addressed
   534	                    </label>
   535	                  </>
   536	                )}
   537	
   538	                {screenshotPreview && (
   539	                  <div style={styles.screenshotPreview}>
   540	                    <img src={screenshotPreview} alt="Screenshot" style={styles.previewImage} />
   541	                    <button
   542	                      style={styles.removeButton}
   543	                      onClick={() => {
   544	                        setScreenshotFile(null)
   545	                        setScreenshotPreview(null)
   546	                      }}
   547	                    >
   548	                      ×
   549	                    </button>
   550	                  </div>
   551	                )}
   552	
   553	                <input
   554	                  ref={fileInputRef}
   555	                  type="file"
   556	                  accept="image/*"
   557	                  style={{ display: 'none' }}
   558	                  onChange={(e) => {
   559	                    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
   560	                    if (file) handleScreenshotSelect(file)
   561	                  }}
   562	                />
   563	
   564	                <div style={styles.actions}>
   565	                  <button
   566	                    style={{ ...styles.actionButton, ...styles.secondaryButton }}
   567	                    onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
   568	                  >
   569	                    📷 Screenshot
   570	                  </button>
   571	                  <button
   572	                    style={{
   573	                      ...styles.actionButton,
   574	                      ...styles.primaryButton,
   575	                      opacity: !message.trim() || isSending ? 0.6 : 1,
   576	                    }}
   577	                    onClick={handleSend}
   578	                    disabled={!message.trim() || isSending}
   579	                  >
   580	                    {isSending ? 'Sending...' : 'Send'}
   581	                  </button>
   582	                </div>
   583	
   584	                <p style={styles.hint}>
   585	                  Tip: Paste (Cmd+V) or drag an image to attach a screenshot
   586	                </p>
   587	              </>
   588	            )}
   589	          </div>
   590	        </div>
   591	      )}
   592	    </div>
   593	  )
   594	}

   596	export default FeedbackWidget

     2	 * AI Service for Feedback Classification

     6	import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from '@google/generative-ai'
     7	import type { FeedbackAIData } from './types'
     8	
     9	export interface AnalyzeFeedbackParams {

    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Analyze feedback with Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite
    17	 * Returns structured AI data or null if AI fails (fail gracefully)
    18	 */
    19	export async function analyzeFeedback({
    20	  messageRaw,
    21	  screenshotBase64,
    22	  geminiApiKey,
    23	}: AnalyzeFeedbackParams): Promise<FeedbackAIData | null> {
    24	  if (!geminiApiKey) {
    25	    console.warn('[OpenFeedbackLayer] No Gemini API key provided, skipping AI analysis')
    26	    return null
    27	  }
    28	
    29	  try {
    30	    const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(geminiApiKey)
    31	    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite' })
    32	
    33	    const prompt = `Analyze this user feedback and classify it. The user is reporting feedback, a bug, feature request, or question.
    34	
    35	User message: "${messageRaw}"
    36	
    37	Extract:
    38	1. A concise title (5-8 words)
    39	2. A short summary (1-2 sentences)
    40	3. Key details as a list
    41	4. Category: bug, feature, question, billing, praise, or other
    42	5. Feature area: which part of the product (e.g. "export", "upload", "dashboard", "prompts", "billing", "login")
    43	6. Priority: low, medium, or high (high = blocking/urgent, medium = important, low = nice to have)
    44	7. Steps to reproduce (if bug)
    45	8. Expected behavior (if bug)
    46	9. Confidence score (0.0-1.0)
    47	10. Clarifying questions (only if really needed, max 2)
    48	
    49	Return ONLY valid JSON (no markdown, no explanation):
    50	{
    51	  "title": "...",
    52	  "short_summary": "...",
    53	  "key_details": ["...", "..."],
    54	  "suggested_category": "bug"|"feature"|"question"|"billing"|"praise"|"other",
    55	  "suggested_feature_area": "...",
    56	  "suggested_priority": "low"|"medium"|"high",
    57	  "steps": ["...", "..."],
    58	  "expected": "..." or null,
    59	  "confidence": 0.0-1.0,
    60	  "clarifying_questions": ["...", "..."]
    61	}`
    62	
    63	    // Build content parts
    64	    const parts: Array<{ text: string } | { inlineData: { mimeType: string; data: string } }> = [
    65	      { text: prompt }
    66	    ]
    67	
    68	    // Add screenshot if provided (Gemini supports vision)
    69	    if (screenshotBase64) {
    70	      const base64Data = screenshotBase64.includes(',')
    71	        ? screenshotBase64.split(',')[1]
    72	        : screenshotBase64
    73	
    74	      let mimeType = 'image/png'
    75	      if (screenshotBase64.startsWith('data:')) {
    76	        const match = screenshotBase64.match(/data:([^;]+)/)
    77	        if (match) {
    78	          mimeType = match[1]
    79	        }
    80	      }
    81	
    82	      parts.push({
    83	        inlineData: {
    84	          mimeType,
    85	          data: base64Data,
    86	        },
    87	      })
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    const result = await model.generateContent(parts)
    91	    const text = result.response.text()
    92	
    93	    if (!text) {
    94	      console.warn('[OpenFeedbackLayer] Empty response from Gemini')
    95	      return null
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    // Extract JSON from response (handle markdown code blocks)
    99	    let jsonText = text.trim()
   100	    const jsonMatch = jsonText.match(/```(?:json)?\s*(\{[\s\S]*\})\s*```/)
   101	    if (jsonMatch) {
   102	      jsonText = jsonMatch[1]
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    const aiData = JSON.parse(jsonText) as FeedbackAIData
   106	
   107	    // Validate structure
   108	    if (
   109	      !aiData.title ||
   110	      !aiData.short_summary ||
   111	      !Array.isArray(aiData.key_details) ||
   112	      !['bug', 'feature', 'question', 'billing', 'praise', 'other'].includes(aiData.suggested_category) ||
   113	      !aiData.suggested_feature_area ||
   114	      !['low', 'medium', 'high'].includes(aiData.suggested_priority)
   115	    ) {
   116	      console.warn('[OpenFeedbackLayer] Invalid AI response structure', aiData)
   117	      return null
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    return aiData
   121	  } catch (error) {
   122	    console.error('[OpenFeedbackLayer] Error analyzing feedback:', error)
   123	    return null // Fail gracefully
   124	  }
   125	}
